## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Full Booleanity supplies a sparse derivative

The complete signed identities sum_S a_S a_(S xor U)=0 for U!=empty, together with sum_S a_S^2=1, are equivalent to f(x)^2=1 at every input. We use that pointwise conclusion. Let

    h_i(x_-i)=[f(+1,x_-i)-f(-1,x_-i)]/2.

Then h_i belongs to {0,+1,-1}; Pr(h_i!=0)=E h_i^2=epsilon_i. Its Fourier coefficient at {j}, j!=i, is a_{ij}. Set

    L_i=sum_{j!=i}a_{ij} x_j,
    d_i=E L_i^2=sum_{j!=i}a_{ij}^2=E[h_i L_i].

For a Rademacher linear form, independence and cosh(u)<=exp(u^2/2) give E exp(t L_i)<=exp(t^2 d_i/2). To verify the scalar inequality, (log cosh u)'=tanh u<=u for u>=0 because (tanh u)'<=1 and tanh 0=0; integrate and use evenness. Thus

    E exp(t|L_i|)<=2 exp(t^2 d_i/2), t>0.

For epsilon_i>0, conditional Jensen on A_i={h_i!=0} yields

    E[|L_i| | A_i] <= ln(2/epsilon_i)/t+t d_i/2.

Indeed, the conditional exponential moment is at most the unconditional moment divided by epsilon_i. Since |h_i|=1 on A_i,

    d_i=E[h_i L_i] <= epsilon_i E[|L_i| | A_i].

If d_i>0, choose t=sqrt(2 ln(2/epsilon_i)/d_i), divide by sqrt(d_i), and square:

    d_i <= 2 epsilon_i^2 ln(2/epsilon_i).          (1)

When epsilon_i=0, h_i is identically zero and d_i=0. When d_i=0 the inequality is immediate. No small-influence assumption is required. The quadratic suppression in (1) is the actual new payment mechanism.

### 2. A normalized influence reference pays the level-two entropy

Assume I>0 and W2>0. For unordered pairs define

    Q({i,j})=2 epsilon_i epsilon_j/I^2,
    P({i,j})=a_{ij}^2/W2.

The reference has total mass 1-sum_i epsilon_i^2/I^2<=1. Every occupied pair has both influences positive, so Q is positive on the support of P. Normalizing Q and applying nonnegative relative entropy gives H(P)<=E_P log2(1/Q). Multiplying by W2 and retaining the level mass entropy yields

    H2 <= sum_i d_i log2(1/epsilon_i)
          + W2 log2(I^2/(2W2)).                  (2)

This step includes the reference normalization; it does not treat the pair masses as a probability law of total mass one. Zero influences contribute zero.

With t=-ln epsilon_i>=0, equation (1) implies

    d_i log2(1/epsilon_i)
      <= epsilon_i * [2 exp(-t)(t^2+t ln2)/ln2].

Calculus gives sup_{t>=0} t^2 exp(-t)=4/e^2 and sup_{t>=0} t exp(-t)=1/e. Therefore

    sum_i d_i log2(1/epsilon_i)
      <= [8/(e^2 ln2)+2/e] I.                    (3)

For every z>0, ln z<=2 sqrt(z)/e: the maximum of ln(z)/sqrt(z) occurs at z=e^2 and is 2/e. Apply this with z=I^2/(2W2). Parseval gives W2<=1, so

    W2 log2(I^2/(2W2))
      <= [sqrt(2)/(e ln2)] I sqrt(W2)
      <= [sqrt(2)/(e ln2)] I.                    (4)

This remains valid even when the logarithm on the left is negative. Combining (2)-(4), and treating W2=0 separately,

    H2 <= K2 I,
    K2=8/(e^2 ln2)+2/e+sqrt(2)/(e ln2).          (5)

### 3. Convert the entropy bill into the requested erasure bill

Every edge has x=a_i^2,y=a_{ij}^2 and x+y<=1 by Parseval. For x,y>0, x ln(1+y/x)<=y and log2((x+y)/y)<=log2(1/y). Hence, with zero cases obtained directly,

    phi(x,y)<=y log2(1/y)+y/ln2.

Each unordered pair appears twice in T12. Also 2W2<=I. Consequently

    T12 <= 2H2+2W2/ln2 <= [2K2+1/ln2] I.        (6)

The following completely rational comparison certifies the constants: e>27/10 since the exponential series through degree five is 163/60; ln2>69/100 since the first two positive atanh terms at 1/3 sum to 56/81; sqrt(2)<71/50 by squaring. Thus

    K2 < 8/[(27/10)^2*(69/100)] + 2/(27/10)
           +(71/50)/[(27/10)*(69/100)]
       =155600/50301,
    2K2+1/ln2 <16700/2187<8.                    (7)

Equations (1)-(7) prove the theorem in all dimensions. No finite census or unknown external theorem is used to extrapolate it.

### 4. Controls identify exactly where Booleanity matters

For the lead's normalized real linear control g=n^(-1/2)sum_i x_i, the spectral derivative energy is 1/n, but the derivative is the nonzero constant 1/sqrt(n): its support probability is one. It violates |ghat(i)|<=spectral influence_i and the support/energy identification in step 1. This control has T12=0, so it tests the mechanism, rather than being a counterexample to the final T12 inequality.

A stronger actual real norm-one control with both levels is

    g_n=(1/sqrt(2n))sum_i x_i
          +(1/sqrt(n(n-1)))sum_{i<j}x_i x_j.

Its first- and second-level masses are both 1/2, so its spectral energy is 3/2. There are n(n-1) oriented edges, each with x=1/(2n), y=1/[n(n-1)]. Since phi(x,y)>=y log2(x/y),

    T12(g_n)>=[log2((n-1)/2)],
    T12(g_n)/I_spectral(g_n)>=(2/3)log2((n-1)/2)->infinity.

This is not a Boolean counterfamily. For U={i}, its signed convolution is 2(n-1)/(sqrt(2n)*sqrt(n(n-1)))>0, violating full Booleanity. It proves that arbitrary norm-one spectra cannot satisfy the proposed universal level-one-to-two payment. The exact constraint, rather than mere normalization, is indispensable.

### 5. Honest remaining line

Let R_ge2(f)=sum_{|S|>=2}sum_{i notin S}phi(a_S^2,a_(S+i)^2). Then

    B(f)=B_root(f)+T12(f)+R_ge2(f).

B20 already pays B_root by [2/(sqrt(e) ln2)]I. This stage pays T12 by (16700/2187)I. The first unsupported universal line is R_ge2(f)<=K I(f) with one dimension-independent K. Repeating a fixed-layer bound with the same whole I at every layer would reuse the budget and does not close this line.

# A097: complete leaf references transferred into physical stopping trees

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite uniform-cube Boolean sign f and every finite deterministic adaptive physical block restriction tree, construct a complete root spectral subprobability reference from arbitrary complete leaf references. A nonempty block A_h is chosen from unqueried coordinates using only the physical history h. Each assignment y has actual conditional probability alpha_h=2^(-|A_h|); no coordinate is queried twice along a path. Different paths may query different blocks. Terminal functions may have arbitrary bias and nonzero entropy. No disjoint selector/data split, orthogonal children, or global order of physical queries is assumed. Entropies, cross-entropies and divergences are in bits; influence uses the ordinary uniform physical flip convention.

At leaf l, fix a nonnegative law Q_l on all subsets of the remaining coordinates, with total mass at most 1, positive on every positive atom of the actual Fourier law P_l. Write c_l=CE(P_l,Q_l)=sum P_l(S) log2(1/Q_l(S)). The generator/dictionary is fixed before sampling a spectral set. These are complete references, including the empty atom; a nonempty-only reference from C020 must first be completed as described below. Let pi_h be the actual physical reach probability.

The new substitution constructs Q_root and proves exactly

    CE(P_root,Q_root) = sum_h pi_h(B_h+J_h-kappa_h) + sum_l pi_l c_l.   (T)

Here B_h is block conditional spectral entropy, J_h the actual physical-child channel information, and kappa_h a specified nonnegative posterior divergence between actual and lifted-reference physical child laws. Consequently

    CE(P_root,Q_root) <= sum_h pi_h(B_h-kappa_h)
                         +(2/ln 2) sum_h pi_h I_A_h(f_h)
                         +sum_l pi_l c_l.                            (P)

This transfers complete references, not just leaf entropy estimates, from C020 into C019/C024. It removes the need to pay a raw physical leaf-label entropy separately. The cumulative erasure term is still unpaid universally. Full FEI remains OPEN. All-dimensional author proof and exact finite checks are supplied; independent acceptance and literature novelty are not claimed.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json in full, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2, and campaign README, CURRENT_STATUS, CLAIM_REGISTRY, OBSTRUCTION_REGISTRY, PACKET_STATUS, STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS and the plan. Relevant plan locators: A097 lines 1126-1130, A028 stopping-tree acceptance, A037 common-law ledger, A039 physical projection and C020 complete-reference costs. The board snapshot at stage start was 2026-10-11 22:05 UTC. The supplied claude/B20/STREAM_NOTES.md was absent; no direction was overridden. Other agents' work was read only.

The latter supplies the block information estimate and exact influence convention. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Actual physical fibers and the new reference generator

At a node suppress h. Let R be the remaining coordinates, A the queried block, C=R\A and m=2^|A|. For U subset A and T subset C put a_U,T=fhat_h(U union T), and let b_y,T be the actual Fourier coefficient of child f_hy at T. Walsh Parseval on A gives

    q(T)=sum_U a_U,T^2 = (1/m) sum_y b_y,T^2.

Write P_y(T)=b_y,T^2. Every P_y is normalized since the child is Boolean. At q(T)>0 define

    theta(U|T)=a_U,T^2/q(T),
    k_P(y|T)=P_y(T)/(m q(T)).

Both are probability kernels. This retains all phases through the actual child coefficients; it is not a universal phase-blind map of parent squared masses. The joint true experiment can be generated bottom-up: choose physical child uniformly, recursively sample its spectral witness with residual set T, then sample U with theta(.|T), independently of y given T. It has parent Fourier law theta(U|T)q(T)=a_U,T^2 and physical child probability 1/m. Inductively it agrees with the common lift of A037.

Given already constructed child references Q_y, put

    r(T)=(1/m) sum_y Q_y(T),
    Q_h(U union T)=theta(U|T) r(T).                       (G)

At q(T)=0, fix any normalized theta(.|T), for example uniform. Such packets have zero true mass, but may carry reference mass: they must not be silently deleted. Formula (G) conserves mass: sum Q_h=(1/m)sum_y sum Q_y <=1. Positivity on actual support follows inductively from leaf support. Thus Q_h is a complete reference on the parent's entire spectral space. References can be chosen differently at different terminal histories; all interior references are determined by (G), rather than reset independently.

### 2. Exact price of the compatible reference transfer

On q(T)>0 define the reference physical-child posterior

    k_Q(y|T)=Q_y(T)/(m r(T)),
    kappa_h=sum_T q(T) D_2(k_P(.|T)||k_Q(.|T)).            (K)

Only positive P_y(T) contribute; support ensures finite logs. Gibbs gives kappa_h>=0 even when Q_y have different total masses. The joint true/reference laws on (y,T) have atoms P_y(T)/m and Q_y(T)/m; the reference can be subnormalized. Expanding their likelihood ratio first by y and then by T gives

    (1/m)sum_y D_2(P_y||Q_y) = D_2(q||r)+kappa_h.         (D)

No reference normalization is discarded. Generalized D(P||Q)=sum P log(P/Q) is used for subprobabilities; it is nonnegative because completing Q with a failure atom reduces to ordinary Gibbs, with P giving that atom zero mass.

Let B_h=H(U|T) under P_h and J_h=H(q)-(1/m)sum_y H(P_y). The restriction identity is H(P_h)=B_h+H(q). The references P_h and Q_h share theta, so D(P_h||Q_h)=D(q||r). Combining with (D) yields

    CE(P_h,Q_h)=B_h+J_h+(1/m)sum_y CE(P_y,Q_y)-kappa_h.

Multiplication by physical reach probabilities and finite telescoping proves (T). The same divergence telescope gives

    D(P_root||Q_root)=sum_l pi_l D(P_l||Q_l)-sum_h pi_h kappa_h.  (S)

In particular sum pi kappa <=sum pi D_leaf. The credit is terminal reference slack that the compatible lift removes; it is not a new source of entropy payment. Choosing every Q_l=P_l makes Q_root=P_root and all kappa_h zero. In that case (T) reduces exactly to the A061 entropy telescope. Arbitrarily replacing an interior Q_h loses (G) and invalidates (T); reference-reset corrections from A037 would then be required.

### 3. Common history and no-reuse certificate

Let L be the complete terminal physical transcript. Define P(S,L) by the bottom-up true generator above, and Q(S,L) by the same physical branch factors 1/m and kernels theta but leaf masses Q_l. Summation over L gives P_root and Q_root. The ratio Q(S,L)/P(S,L), on true support, is exactly Q_l(S_remaining)/P_l(S_remaining): the physical branch and innovation factors cancel. Thus joint divergence is sum pi_l D_leaf. Expanding the joint divergence by S and using (S) proves

    E_{S~P_root} D_2(P(L|S)||Q(L|S)) = sum_h pi_h kappa_h.

The reference posterior Q(L|S) is normalized for each positive root spectral atom, even though the full Q(S,L) may be subnormalized. This is one global posterior correction, consumed once. It is not a sum of separately recharged root credits. Similarly the true law satisfies

    I_P(S;L)=sum_h pi_h J_h,
    H_P(L|S)=H_P(L)-sum_h pi_h J_h.

These follow directly from the function-specific common lift: given current residual T and history, the next physical outcome is independent of every already erased label. Thus the apparent physical path-label entropy H(L) in an A011-style witness is reduced by its posterior redundancy to sum pi J. The new construction carries complete leaf references through that cancellation, including their support and mass, rather than only the true Fourier laws.

### 4. Explicit dimension-independent channel price

The uniform cube log-Sobolev inequality in the needed natural normalization is

    Ent(g^2) <= (1/2)sum_i E(g(x)-g(x^i))^2, g>=0.

For completeness the two-point assertion is F(t)>=0, where
F(t)=(t^2+1)ln((t^2+1)/2)-2t^2 ln t+(t-1)^2. F(1)=0. For t>=1 its derivative is nonnegative because ln(2t^2/(t^2+1))<=1-1/t; the difference's derivative is (t-1)^2/[t^2(t^2+1)]>=0. Symmetry F(t)=t^2 F(1/t) and continuity handle all endpoints. Entropy tensorization follows by conditioning successively on independent coordinates, giving the stated product inequality.

Apply it to g_y=|b_y,T|/sqrt(q(T)), for each positive fiber T, and sum with weights q(T). The left side is (ln 2)J_h. Replacing magnitude differences by signed differences only increases the right side. Parseval on C gives

    J_h <= (1/(2 ln 2))sum_{i in A} E_y sum_T
                              (b_y,T-b_(y^i),T)^2
        = (2/ln 2) I_A(f_h).

The Boolean signs give squared physical difference 4 on pivotal edges. This proves the constant with the exact physical normalization. Influence conservation is I(f_h)=I_A(f_h)+(1/m)sum_y I(f_hy); hence I(f)=I_removed+I_terminal, where I_removed=sum pi_h I_A(f_h), I_terminal=sum pi_l I(f_l). No influence is spent twice. Substitution gives (P).

### 5. Precisely closed conditional endpoint and exact remaining term

If complete leaf references satisfy c_l<=C_leaf I(f_l) and this actual tree satisfies

    sum_h pi_h(B_h-kappa_h)<=K I_removed,

for finite K,C_leaf>=0, then the constructed root reference satisfies

    H(f)<=CE(P_root,Q_root)<=max(K+2/ln 2,C_leaf) I(f).

This conclusion is all-dimensional under its stated hypotheses. No existence/payment theorem for that tree is claimed. The amount newly paid is the entire channel/history information, at at most (2/ln 2)I_removed; compatible-reference mismatch receives the exact credit sum pi kappa, bounded by leaf slack. The remaining universal line is the displayed erasure-minus-credit inequality together with uniformly certified complete leaf costs. This is not asserted by an analogy or by KL positivity.

For a nonempty subprobability reference R_l from C020 let v_l=P_l(S nonempty). Its complete extension is Q_l(empty)=1-v_l and Q_l(S)=v_l R_l(S) for nonempty S. Then

    CE(P_l,Q_l)=h_2(v_l)+sum_{S nonempty}P_l(S)log2(1/R_l(S)).

For v_l=0 choose Q_l=delta_empty; for v_l=1 set the empty mass to zero. Therefore any complete witness bound from the normalized-prior lemma can be inserted verbatim, retaining its h_2(v), root normalizer, scale prior and all gate/rank costs. Optimizing those terms independently is not permitted. Conversely an entropy-only terminal theorem permits Q_l=P_l for an existence argument, but supplies no computationally cheap dictionary claim.

The accepted B05 all-coordinate local-refund obstruction still applies: B_h/I_A can grow without bound at a chosen rare selector. This transfer does not make every split inexpensive. It provides a compatible construction for prescribed leaf laws and keeps the costly conditional labels explicit.

# A097 stage 2: B05 leaf references inserted into the compatible telescope

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Execute B20 STREAM_NOTES v2's A097 target: compute the exact control-first tree residual sum_h pi_h(B_h-kappa_h)/I_removed on the actual B05 activated bent-defect family for k=2,4,6,8. The B_h here is the queried coordinate's conditional spectral entropy at its actual restriction node; it is not the aggregate C018 B(f). All information uses bits and ordinary uniform physical influence.

For even k>=2 let delta=2^-k, e=2^(-k/2), r=2k and p=2^-r. The actual quadratic bent seed is b(x)=(-1)^(x1x2+...+x_(k-1)x_k), its one-point flip is b'=b-2*1_zero, h is their fair physical-selector splice, and F=1+A_r(h-1). All functions take values +-1. The physical tree queries the fresh point controls in their fixed order, stops inactive at the first mismatch, and otherwise queries the selector and stops at b or b'. The original B05 local no-go used even k>=4; k=2 is a legitimate additional Boolean calibration member.

Two prescribed complete reference modes are compared. E assigns the actual squared Fourier law to each seed leaf. U assigns the same uniform bent-spectrum reference Q(T)=delta to both seed leaves. All constant leaves use delta_empty. References include the empty atom and are fixed before the spectral sample; no interior reoptimization occurs. The A097 v1 compatible lift determines every interior and root law.

This stage supplies exact finite expressions with outward rational certificates, an all-even-k posterior-credit cap, an all-even-k control-first erasure bound B_tree<=3 I_removed, and the complete root-reference consequence CE<= (3+2/ln2) I. The family already has FEI certificates from A023/A064, so this is a reference-transfer/application certificate and mechanism diagnosis, not a new FEI class or universal constant. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md contains the full proof. CROSS_ROUTE_SUBSTITUTION.tex contains the corresponding theorem and formulas. The essential exact substitution follows.

There are N_+=(2^k+2^(k/2))/2 positive and N_-=(2^k-2^(k/2))/2 negative bent Walsh coefficients sigma e. The flip subtracts 2delta from all coefficients. At a data index of sign sigma the core selector coefficients are sigma e-delta and delta; its marginal is q_sigma=delta-2sigma delta e+2delta^2. The core mean is mu=e-delta and the activation coefficient is c=1-mu in [3/4,1).

The selector erasure is B_sel=sum_sigma N_sigma phi((sigma e-delta)^2,delta^2). Let H_b' be the exact entropy of masses (sigma e-2delta)^2 with these multiplicities. The selector's channel information is J_sel=H(q)-[k+H_b']/2. Under reference E, kappa_sel=0 and Q_h(empty)=mu^2. Under reference U, the two leaf references are identical, their reference physical posterior is fair, and kappa_sel=J_sel exactly; Q_h(empty)=delta mu^2/q_+.

At an active node with s controls remaining, u_s=2^-s, physical reach pi_s=p/u_s and d_s=u_s c. B20's activation coefficient formula gives the actual empty residual packet masses (1-d_s)^2,d_s^2, with q_s=1-2d_s+2d_s^2. Its control-coordinate conditional entropy is

    B_s=phi((1-d_s)^2,d_s^2)+(1-q_s).

This is precisely the y-coordinate summand of B20 (A): u(1-mu^2)+(u-2u^2)c^2+phi, using 1-mu^2+c^2=2c. Thus its measure, bias, normalizers and bit units match the target telescope. It is not an analogy to a balanced core.

Writing z_(s-1) for the active-child reference's empty mass, A097 v1 determines

    z_s=((1-d_s)^2/q_s)(1+z_(s-1))/2,
    w_s=(d_s^2/q_s)(1+z_(s-1))/2,
    kappa_s=q_s D_2((1,(1-2d_s)^2)/(2q_s) || (1,z_(s-1))/(1+z_(s-1))).

Only the empty residual packet contributes this posterior KL. Every nonempty packet has true and reference physical posteriors concentrated on the active child. All reference laws remain normalized and positive on true support.

The exact requested ratio is therefore

    R_k = [B_sel-kappa_sel + sum_(s=1)^(2k) (B_s-kappa_s)/u_s]
          / [2k c+delta].

These rational/logarithmic expressions constitute the exact values. The following decimals summarize certified rational intervals; they are not floating-point proof premises.

| k | B_tree/I_removed (also R_k under E) | K_tree/I_removed under U | R_k under U |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| 2 | 2.529559232017 | 0.177128026958 | 2.352431205059 |
| 4 | 2.348254434608 | 0.027709745516 | 2.320544689093 |
| 6 | 2.222200785224 | 0.004241766778 | 2.217959018446 |
| 8 | 2.160400273400 | 0.000749758065 | 2.159650515335 |

out/RESULTS.json retains exact rational lower/upper endpoints, every node's charge/reference empty mass, terminal KL, root CE and full entropy/influence ratios. The correct influence accounts are

    I_removed=p(2k c+delta),
    I_terminal=p(k/2+kdelta),
    I(F)=I_removed+I_terminal.

The complete telescope keeps p times the actual average leaf entropy under E and p*k under U. Under U the terminal KL slack is p[k-H_b']/2. The independent closed root reference, grouped by deepest included control frequency, verifies its mass is exactly one and K_tree=p[k-H_b']/2-D_root. The direct integer-Walsh check compares every root reference atom at k=2,4 with that compressed formula. Interval overlap is merely an implementation check: the exact equality follows from the stated rational law and A097's proved chain rule.

The mechanism has a quantitative limit. The point-flipped bent spectral law satisfies chi-square(P_b'||uniform)=16delta(1-delta), exactly. Since ln t<=t-1 and the A097 posterior correction cannot exceed terminal reference slack,

    0<=K_tree/I_removed
      <=8delta(1-delta)/[(2k c+delta)ln2] = O(2^-k/k).

Thus these inexpensive seed references provide exponentially negligible credit. Increasing artificial terminal slack is not a free repair: it raises the terminal reference bill.

For all even k>=2 the erasure rate is uniformly paid on this fixed tree. Indeed phi(a,b)<=b log2(e_math/b) when a+b<=1, so

    B_tree/p <=4k c+C0+delta[k+log2(e_math)],
    C0=log2(e_math)+4+2log2(4/3)<13/2.

For k>=6, 2k c>=3k/2>=9 and delta(k+3/2)<=15/128, yielding B_tree/I_removed<3. The certified k=2,4 intervals supply the remaining cases. A lower bound B_tree/p>=4k c-2 proves B_tree/I_removed tends to 2, and the credit cap proves R_k under U also tends to 2. Each prescribed seed reference costs at most 2I(seed), so the actual complete root reference obeys

    CE(P_F,Q_F)<=(3+2/ln2)I_removed+2I_terminal
              <=(3+2/ln2)I(F).

This is the closed scoped C019 application. The first unsupported universal line is unchanged: arbitrary Boolean seed references and colliding histories must admit a dimension-independent payment of sum pi(B-kappa) together with their entire terminal costs. There is no such proof here. Further samples of this already bounded-ratio family would calibrate the same mechanism, not test full FEI.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**Independent global proof.** At an interior node with physical block A, let P_y(T) be the actual child squared Fourier law and q(T)=2^(-|A|) sum_y P_y(T). Parent Parseval gives P_h(U,T)=theta_h(U|T)q(T). Generate a joint law P(S,L) bottom-up: choose the physical child uniformly, recursively draw its spectral set, and restore the erased spectral label U with the actual kernel theta, independently of y given T. The root spectral marginal is P_0 and the terminal history L has its actual physical reach weights pi_l. Generate the subprobability Q(S,L) by using exactly the same physical transitions and theta kernels and replacing only each terminal P_l by Q_l. At zero true fibers choose a normalized theta, retaining any reference mass there.

On every positive true path, all physical and restoration factors cancel. Thus Q(S,L)/P(S,L)=Q_l(T_l)/P_l(T_l). Consequently

    D_2(P(S,L)||Q(S,L)) = sum_l pi_l D_2(P_l||Q_l) = D_L.

Marginalizing to S and applying the finite KL chain rule gives

    D_L = D_0 + E_S D_2(P(L|S)||Q(L|S)) = D_0 + K_tot.       (1)

Q may be subnormalized, but Q(L|S) is normalized on each positive true root atom, and generalized D_0>=0 by normalizing Q_0 and retaining minus log2 its total mass. Hence 0<=K_tot<=D_L. A one-node chain rule of the physical child/residual-set joint law identifies the local posterior correction kappa_h; induction gives K_tot=sum_h pi_h kappa_h. This agrees with A097's correction using a global likelihood reconstruction rather than its CE telescope.

Since D_L=C_L-H_L, equation (1) proves the stronger complete-cost bookkeeping identity

    C_L-K_tot = H_L+D_0 >= H_L.                              (2)

If the complete terminal costs obey C_L<=C I_L, then

    K_tot <= C I_L-H_L <= C I_L.                             (3)

For zero-cost constant terminals, C_L=H_L=I_L=0, so K_tot=D_0=0. In particular, restricting all the way to constants with their paid references cannot finance conditional erasure using terminal KL. Artificially shrinking a leaf law creates credit only with a corresponding complete CE charge; (2) retains that charge exactly. If a sequence of terminal costs satisfies one fixed C and I_L tends to zero in expectation, then K_tot tends to zero as well. This latter assertion requires the stated expectation convergence, not almost-sure terminal convergence across dimensions; A062's obstruction precludes that substitution.

Combine (2) with the A097 transfer identity, reconstructed by the same one-node spectral entropy chain rule. With B_tot=sum pi_h B_h and J_tot=sum pi_h J_h,

    CE(P_0,Q_0) = B_tot+J_tot-K_tot+C_L
                = B_tot+J_tot+H_L+D_0
                = H(f)+D_0.                                (4)

Therefore if a proposed proof promises B_tot-K_tot<=K I_removed and C_L<=C I_L, it must also satisfy

    B_tot+H_L <= K I_removed+C I_L.                         (5)

The source block-channel bound already prices J_tot by (2/ln2)I_removed. Neither (1)-(5) supplies universal selection or pays B_tot. These statements diagnose which credit is available; they do not refute stopping trees, references, or FEI. The first unsupported universal line remains: construct a history-measurable proper stopping rule and complete terminal laws with B_tot-K_tot<=K I_removed and C_L<=C I_L for fixed dimension-independent K,C. Choosing Q_l=P_l returns Q_0=P_0 and CE=H(f), so mere leaf-law optimization is not an independent payment mechanism.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**Why these six retirements are justified.** For any Boolean squared spectrum, conditioning and the chain rule give sum_i B_i<=H. The influence-weighted average then gives min_{i:Inf_i>0} B_i/Inf_i<=sum_i B_i/I<=H/I. Irrelevant coordinates have B_i=0 and are excluded from division. Thus a minimum-ratio search on a bounded-H/I family calibrates a rule and cannot alone supply a diverging FEI counterfamily. This existing observation is reused only as the retirement criterion, not renamed as new mathematics. The B05 calibration has already been solved by A001/A040/A089 and is archived. A082 remains the one certificate/rule lane, with its actual terminal-cost uses intact.

The half-tax endpoint is also established, with A090's independent reconstruction of A096's uniform theorem and explicit Boolean lower witness. Three future duplicate reviews are retired. A045's unequal-bias composition, A046's literal all-p correction and incomplete sharp finite-p certificate, and A073's general exact logarithm engine remain distinct reusable outputs. Keeping A090 does not turn the sharper B10 full finite-p or multi-bit claim into an accepted theorem. No historical result is deleted and no active process is canceled.

**Independent all-dimensional review of the next closed layer.** Put epsilon_i=Inf_i(f) and h_i=(f(x_i=+1)-f(x_i=-1))/2 on the residual uniform cube. Full Booleanity implies h_i is 0 or +/-1, its nonzero support has probability epsilon_i, and E h_i^2=epsilon_i. Let L_i=sum_{j!=i} a_{ij}x_j and d_i=E L_i^2=E h_i L_i.

A Rademacher linear form has E exp(tL_i)<=exp(t^2d_i/2), because cosh u<=exp(u^2/2); consequently E exp(t|L_i|)<=2exp(t^2d_i/2). This scalar inequality follows by integrating tanh u<=u and using evenness. Let mu_A be the uniform law conditioned on the support A of h_i. Its relative entropy to the unconditional law is ln(1/epsilon_i). The elementary entropy variational inequality, obtained by Jensen applied to the likelihood ratio inside exp(t|L_i|), gives

    E_A |L_i| <= [ln(1/epsilon_i)+ln E exp(t|L_i|)]/t
               <= ln(2/epsilon_i)/t+t d_i/2.

Since d_i<=epsilon_i E_A |L_i|, optimization in t and squaring prove d_i<=2 epsilon_i^2 ln(2/epsilon_i). Zero support or zero row energy is handled separately. This is the critical Boolean step; energy equals actual support probability only for the signed Boolean derivative.

Independently use an ordered-pair reference q(i,j)=epsilon_i epsilon_j/I^2 for i!=j. Its total mass is at most one. If W2=sum_{i<j}a_{ij}^2>0, the normalized actual ordered-pair law is p(i,j)=a_{ij}^2/(2W2). Gibbs for a subprobability q, retaining the mass 2W2, yields exactly

    H2 <= sum_i d_i log2(1/epsilon_i)
           +W2 log2(I^2/(2W2)).                         (1)

With t=-ln epsilon_i, the derivative bound and sup t exp(-t)=1/e, sup t^2 exp(-t)=4/e^2 imply the first term is at most [8/(e^2 ln2)+2/e]I. The scalar maximum of ln z/sqrt(z) is 2/e, so the second term is at most [sqrt(2)/(e ln2)]I sqrt(W2), and W2<=1. This argument also covers negative logarithms: it is an upper inequality for every z>0, not a positive-log truncation.

For x+y<=1, x ln(1+y/x)<=y and log2((x+y)/y)<=log2(1/y) give phi(x,y)<=y log2(1/y)+y/ln2. Each level-two atom is counted twice, and 2W2<=I. Therefore

    T12 <= 2H2+2W2/ln2 <= (2K2+1/ln2)I,
    K2=8/(e^2 ln2)+2/e+sqrt(2)/(e ln2).

The exact lower comparisons e>27/10 and ln2>69/100 follow respectively from the exponential series through degree five (163/60) and the first two positive atanh terms (56/81). The upper comparison sqrt(2)<71/50 follows by squaring. Substitution gives K2<155600/50301 and 2K2+1/ln2<16700/2187<8. This independently reconstructs the entire stated fragment without using A096's checker, finite extrapolation, or any pending universal helper. There is no missing line in the scoped target.

The explicit real normalized control with first-level masses 1/(2n) and second-level masses 1/[n(n-1)] has spectral energy 3/2 and T12>=log2((n-1)/2). It is not Boolean: its signed singleton convolution is strictly positive. Its diverging (2/3)log2((n-1)/2) lower ratio proves that this proof's sparse-derivative requirement cannot be discarded. It is not an FEI counterexample.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**Independent orbit minimax reconstruction.** Let P_pi be the squared Fourier law of a coordinate permutation, and let barP average these laws over all permutations. For any subprobability Q of mass z<=1, linearity and Gibbs give

    max_pi CE(P_pi,Q) >= CE(barP,Q)
                     = H(barP)+D(barP||Q/z)-log2(z) >= H(barP).

Zero mass on a positive barP atom gives infinite CE and is handled directly. The averaged law is uniform within each degree: barP(S)=W_|S|/binom(n,|S|). Every P_pi has identical degree totals W_d. Consequently CE(P_pi,barP)=H(barP) for every pi, and Q=barP is admissible. Thus the exact minimax identity is

    inf_{Q: sum Q<=1} max_pi CE(P_pi,Q)
       = H(barP) = H(W)+sum_d W_d log2 binom(n,d).        (1)

This reconstructs the obstruction independently and also identifies its exact reference optimum. It does not assume Q itself is exchangeable; symmetry enters the averaged actual Boolean laws. For influence-only rules the reference is held fixed across this orbit, since each coordinate's influence is the same and permutation-invariant metadata are unchanged.

**All-width Boolean lower bound.** Write delta=1/k and A_j for each block point indicator. For f=1-2[1-prod_j(1-A_j)], every Fourier atom supported on a nonempty subset T of exactly one block has squared magnitude 4 delta^2 (1-delta)^{2(k-1)}. There are k binom(w,m) such atoms of degree m. Disjoint block supports prevent double counting. Hence the mass in these atoms with m>=ceil(w/2) is

    4k delta^2 a sum_{m>=ceil(w/2)} binom(w,m),
    a=(1-1/k)^{2(k-1)}.

The binomial sum is at least 2^{w-1} by complementary pairing. Integrating 1/(1-t)<=1/(1-u) on [0,u] gives ln(1-u)>=-u/(1-u), so a>=exp(-2)>1/9. The latter follows from e<3: j!>=2^{j-1} for j>=2, strict at j=3, bounds the exponential series by 3. Thus the specified spectral mass is at least 2/9.

For these degrees, binom(n,m)=prod_{j=0}^{m-1}(n-j)/(m-j)>=(n/m)^m>=k^m, since m<=w. Therefore log2 binom(n,m)>=wm>=w^2/2. All other degree-location terms and H(W) are nonnegative. Equation (1) is at least w^2/9.

A coordinate is pivotal exactly when its other w-1 block bits are positive and every other block fails. Its influence is 2delta(1-delta)^{k-1}, equal for all n coordinates. Consequently I=2w(1-delta)^{k-1}<=2w. Combining these identities gives, for some coordinate permutation and every common Q,

    CE(P_pi,Q)/I >= w/18.

All quantities come from actual Boolean functions. No finite-width extrapolation is used: the preceding derivation holds for every integer w>=2. The independent record accepts this clause without repair. It does not accept the other three clauses bundled in A001@1, and is deliberately excluded from full-claim queue acceptance.

**Current universal frontier.** A096's T12 payment was already independently accepted by A098 stage2. A090's parity-tail low-influence completeness is now independently accepted by A096. A073 has independently accepted A096's terminal-inclusive refund and demonstrated that conditioning Q on derivative support removes the activation refund. These are consumed, not rederived here. The first unpaid line for the complete joint reference P(i,T)=p_(T+i)/I, Q(i,T)=(e_i/I)(p_T+p_(T+i)) is

    D(P_f||Q_f) >= sum_i (e_i/I)log2(1/e_i) - K          (OPEN)

for one finite K and every terminal Boolean f. Equivalently M<=KI, with M<=B<=M+I/ln2. The root and T12 erasure layers are paid; R_ge2 remains unpaid. The orbit obstruction excludes a complete spectral reference based only on influence data; it does not exclude this function-specific conditional-edge reference or retained-KL payment. Activation refunds its own rarity while retaining the arbitrary seed bill.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The independent complete derivation below is saved separately as DERIVATION.md. A098 supplies no repair to A073@1 and is eligible as independent reviewer.

**Independent reconstruction.**

All logarithms are base two. Fix even d>=2, delta=2^-d, t=sqrt(delta)=2^(-d/2), e=delta/2. Put b(y)=(-1)^(y1*y2+...+y(d-1)*yd), q(u,v)=-1 only at (1,1), and F=b+1[y=0](q-1). It is Boolean since b(0)=1. Use 0/1 physical labels and characters (-1)^(S dot x).

The two-bit bent factor has coefficients (1,1,1,-1)/2, so b has coefficients sigma_T*t. Their sum is b(0)=1, giving N_+-N_-=1/t and N_++N_-=1/delta. The coefficients of q are (1,1,1,-1)/2. Therefore the four F amplitudes over each core set T are sigma_T*t-delta/2, delta/2, delta/2, -delta/2. They are nonzero since t<=1/2 and t>delta/2. Every squared Fourier atom is positive and both queried erasures are strictly positive.

Consider query u and let V denote whether the residual set contains v. On V=0, the two paired masses are (sigma*t-delta/2)^2 and delta^2/4; on V=1 both are delta^2/4. The upper mass totals e=delta/2. Thus the complete derivative law mu(T,V) is delta/2 for every T,V. The complete reference r(T,V), which includes all residual atoms, is delta*c_sigma on V=0 and delta^2/2 on V=1, where c_sigma=1-sigma*t+delta/2. Summing yields r_V=(1-e,e), while mu_V=(1/2,1/2). These are complete normalized laws, with no derivative-support conditioning.

Given V=1, both conditional core laws are uniform, with each atom delta. Given V=0, the derivative law remains uniform and the reference has masses delta*c_sigma/(1-e). KL chain decomposition gives

    D(mu||r) = D((1/2,1/2)||(1-e,e)) + D0/2,
    D0 = sum_sigma N_sigma*delta*log2((1-e)/c_sigma).

Thus quotient loss in influence-weighted divergence is L_u=e*D0/2. Symmetry gives the same result for v. Both sigma values occur with positive counts, and their c values differ, so strict KL nonnegativity gives D0>0.

For the upper bound, c_- >= c_+. Moreover

    7*c_+ - 5*(1-e) = (2-3t)*(1-2t) >= 0.

Every conditional likelihood ratio is therefore at most 7/5, implying D0<=log2(7/5). The exact integer inequality 7^128 < 2^63*5^128 gives log2(7/5)<63/128. Consequently

    0 < L_u/e <= log2(7/5)/2 < 63/256 = 1/4-1/256.

The two losses together are e*D0<delta/4. Each core coordinate has influence (1+delta)/2: the original bent influence is 1/2, all edges from 0 to a basis vector were nonpivotal, and the edit reverses those edges for exactly one quarter of the gate assignments, adding delta/2. Because delta<=1/4,

    delta/4 <= (1+delta)/20 = Inf_y1/10.

Strictness in the first inequality proves the stated dedicated reserve bound, including d=2 where the second inequality is equality. This fraction pays only these two losses, once; no entropy or other row charge is assigned to it.

The complete retained row price is not bounded by this remainder estimate. Direct substitution of the binary KL gives

    M_u/e = 1 + (1/2)log2((1-e)/e) - D0/2
          > d/2 + 3/4,

since (1-e)/e=2^(d+1)-1>=2^d and D0/2<1/4. Summing actual physical influences gives I=d/2+(d/2+1)*delta. The spectrum lives on d+2 coordinates, so H<=d+2 and H/I<=2+4/d<=4. None of these functions disproves FEI.

This proves every clause of A073-NONZERO-ERASURE-BINARY-REMAINDER@1 without modifying its statement or adding a repair. A098 is an independent reviewer of this version. Its analytic quantifiers cover all even d>=2; the five fixed-dimensional truth tables independently verify the coefficient and conditional-law identities, not an extrapolation. The universal first unsupported line remains a complete normalized conditional partition and globally nonoverlapping terminal reserve for arbitrary Boolean rows, sufficient to pay sum_i e_i*D_i >= sum_i e_i*log2(1/e_i)-K*I with one absolute K.

The checker represents KL as rational linear forms in logarithms of integer primes. Differences of predicted and complete-law expressions cancel exactly, before any interval estimate. For signs, it reduces positive rational log arguments to a power of two times x in [1,2), then uses z=(x-1)/(x+1) and 12 fixed terms of 2*sum z^(2j+1)/(2j+1). The positive remainder is bounded by 2*z^25/(25*(1-z^2)); division by the positive analogous ln2 interval preserves rigorous endpoints. No floating logarithm or repeated precision search is used.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**Independent complete reconstruction.**

All costs are ideal bits on uniform physical cubes. Put n=a+b, s=2^-a, t=2^-n, p=2^-r, mu=1-2s, rho=4pt, lambda=2p*2^-b. At a zero point the actual sign is F=1-2A_a+2A_rD_n. Translating the active/control/dummy point changes Fourier signs but preserves probabilities, physical influences and this construction.

An active data coordinate has original influence 2s and loses 2pt; each dummy coordinate gains 2pt; each control coordinate has influence 2pt. Thus I_g=2as, C=2ptb, E=2pta, J=2rpt and I_F=I_g+C-E+J. In particular E/I_g=p*2^-b<=1/2. These are actual edge changes, including destruction, not only the net influence increase.

Let P be the source spectrum. Its amplitudes are mu at empty, -2s on nonempty active-only sets and zero elsewhere. Summing the control spectral atoms of the point edit gives q_T=P_T+rho*ghat(T)+rho*t. Since rho=2s*lambda, this is precisely

    q=(1-lambda)P + lambda*mu*delta_empty + rho*U_n.

The weights sum to one and are nonnegative; rho>0 supplies positive mass on every data set. This identity is reconstructed directly for all widths rather than invoking the broader A086 floor theorem.

For any normalized supplied R with finite source cost c, calibrate R0(empty)=mu^2 and preserve its conditional nonempty law. Its cost c0=c-D_Ber(mu^2||R(empty)) is at most c. Finite c excludes an all-vacuum R because P has positive nonempty mass. The calibrated law may still vanish off source support. Set Rprime to the same three-component mixture with P replaced by R0, and use Q(U,T)=Rprime(T)*P_F(U,T)/q(T). It is a complete probability law; off-source reference zeros are covered by its uniform floor. True conditional output zeros have zero Q and do not incur false success mass.

In the augmented label/data distributions use identical weights and components (P,delta_empty,U)/(R0,delta_empty,U). Their joint KL is (1-lambda)D(P||R0). Disintegration over the observed data set T gives

    D(P_F||Q)=D(q||Rprime)=(1-lambda)D(P||R0)-kappa,

where kappa is the actual posterior-label KL for this same reference. No generic replacement or renewed posterior supplies that credit.

For a>=2, mu>=1/2 and t<=s<=1/4. Empty calibration makes q0=Rprime0=mu^2+rho(mu+t). Put v=1-mu^2, A=(1-lambda)v, B=rho(1-t), M=A+B=1-q0<=v. The nonempty true/reference conditional laws mix the original source conditional and the uniform nonempty law with weights A/M,B/M. Applying the elementary complete-reference mixture inequality to these two labels, and subtracting c0, gives

    CE(q,Rprime)-c0 <= L(q0)-L(mu^2)+L(M)-L(v)
                       +B*log2(2^n-1)+phi(A,B).

The discarded source conditional term is (A-v)*CE(P_positive,R0_positive)<=0, so its removal has the correct sign. Here L(x)=-x log2 x and phi(A,B)=M*h2(B/M). Since q0>=mu^2>=1/4, L'(x)<=1 on the vacuum interval; q0-mu^2=rho(mu+t)<=rho. On (0,1], L'(x)>=-1/ln2, yielding L(M)-L(v)<=rho/ln2. The sum is therefore less than 4rho. Continuous zero limits cover boundary sectors.

We have v<=4s, B>=3rho/4 and M/B<=(4/3)*2^(b+r). The elementary binary bound h2(x)<=x log2(e/x) gives phi(A,B)<=rho*(b+r+2), using log2(4/3)<1/2 and 1/ln2<3/2. The local subset bill is at most rho*n. Hence CE(q,Rprime)<=c+rho*(n+b+r+6).

Every nonempty control set has marginal mass 4p^2t, so their combined probability V=rho*(1-p). Therefore the conditional control entropy is at most

    H(S_controls)=h2(V)+V*log2(2^r-1)
       <=rho*(1-p)*(log2(e/rho)+r)<=rho*(n+2r).

The cancellation of log2(1-p) is retained; log2(e)<2 supplies the last inequality. Combining costs and using b<=n,n+r>=3 gives

    CE_F<=c+rho*(2n+b+3r+6)<=c+5rho*(n+r)
         =c+10(C+E+J).

For a=1, mu=0 and lambda=rho, so the data mixture has only source and uniform components. Directly CE(q,Rprime)<=c+rho*n+h2(rho)<=c+rho*(2n+r); adding control entropy gives c+3rho*(n+r)=c+6(C+E+J), which implies the stated bound even at n=r=1. The constants follow from ln2>2/3 and ln2<1: the positive atanh series gives ln2>2/3, while integrating 1/x<1 on (1,2) gives ln2<1. Squaring 4/3 proves log2(4/3)<1/2. No unreviewed constant estimate is used.

For every K>=10 subtract this complete cost from K I_F:

    K I_F-CE_F >= (K I_g-c)+(K-10)C-(K+10)E+(K-10)J.

If c<=C0 I_g, set K=2C0+10. Created/control coefficients are nonnegative and E<=I_g/2; the remaining source-minus-erasure bank is at least [K-C0-(K+10)/2]I_g=0. This spends the bank once. Structural R=P has H_g<4I_g: for a>=2, v=4s(1-s), L(mu^2)<=v/ln2 and the other contribution is v*(2a-2), strictly less than 8as. For a=1 the entropy is zero. Thus this structural floor has CE_F<=18I_F.

For any independent Boolean terminal h with complete finite-cost R_h, product probabilities and physical influences give exactly CE_total=c_h+CE_F, D_total=D_h+(1-lambda)D(P||R0)-kappa, and I_total=I_h+I_F. The terminal cost is retained with coefficient one and supplies no floor reserve. If c_h<=C_h I_h, payment follows at max(C_h,2C0+10).

This reconstructs all clauses of A097-NEGATIVE-CELL-FLOOR-SOURCE-BANK@1 without repairing them. It does not accept A086's broader subprobability/72-tax theorem. The first unpaid hereditary line remains transporting the incoming Q through an actual active-bit restriction and paying child normalizers/reset from the same unused bank. Closure of the Boolean formula family does not imply closure of an arbitrary supplied reference; the point-edit floor must not be freshly financed at every descendant.


**Where the next source-bound proof must begin.** For a declared signed-amplitude lift a_S=epsilon_S sqrt(Q_S) of an incoming normalized spectral reference, the physical cofactor amplitudes are a_T +/- a_(T+i). Their normalization factors are Z_+=1+2 sum_T a_T a_(T+i) and Z_-=1-2 sum_T a_T a_(T+i). They average to1 and lie in[0,2], but need not both be1 as the actual Boolean source's cofactors are. A lift and its actual child normalizers must therefore be specified before treating descendant references as fresh normalized floors. This elementary diagnostic is not a construction of paid descendants: phase choice, possible zero child norm, complete terminal debt and the same remaining bank are exactly the next unresolved interface. Mixture labels in the floor are analytical labels and cannot silently become fair physical child histories.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

For arbitrary nonconstant f, h_m=z*f^(tensor m) has mean zero, B(h_m)=mB(f), I(h_m)=mI(f)+1. A point activation at k controls has exact I=2^(-k)[mI(f)+1+k] and B_data=2^(-k)mB(f) plus two explicitly nonnegative root-packet sums. Thus B(F)>=2^(-k)mB(f). With sbar(N)=max_(M<=N)s(M) and k_m=ceil(sbar(2mn)+log2(3mn))+1, the constructed N_m=mn+1+k_m cube meets I<=2^(-sbar(N_m)) for large m and k_m/m->0. Dividing an assumed low-influence bound by m*2^(-k_m) proves B(f)<=K_E(sbar)I(f), hence K_E(s)=K_E in the extended reals. This removes the prior additive root price 2/(sqrt(e)ln2), and the C018 corollary is C_FEI<=K_E(s)+2/ln2. Neither finiteness nor a chosen-coordinate witness is assumed or established.

The original version-1 additive theorem remains true; version 2 strengthens it. A005's counterexample concerns a cheap CONTROL witness, which does not invalidate a lower bound on the entire aggregate B. Fresh balancing can create deterministic spectral membership on z, whose B_z is zero; its one influence unit is retained until the limit. Tensor and activation must not be confused with unconditional coordinate-witness transfer.

The revised universal conjecture is R_ge2(f)<=K I(f), with all n and all exact signed Boolean convolution identities quantified. Root and singleton-to-pair layers are paid at c_root=2/(sqrt(e)ln2) and c12=16700/2187; full FEI would follow with K+c_root+c12+2/ln2. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

CANDIDATE_MANUSCRIPT.tex is the complete single source, with previous proved lemmas retained and three new sections containing full proofs. ADDITIONS.tex records the stage's additions, but is not an external TeX input.

For any seed f, m disjoint copies, ell>=2 fresh parity coordinates and k point controls, write h=chi_Z*f^tensor(m), F=1+A_k(h-1), q=2^-k. Occupied core frequencies contain Z, so all seed erasure edges lie above the deleted layers and each fresh parity bit has zero erasure and influence q. Direct occupied-pair accounting gives

    I(F)=q[mI(f)+ell+k],
    R_geell(F)=qmB(f)+kq+2kq^2 sum_(r=ell..k-1) binom(k-1,r),
    R_ge2(F)=qmB(f)+2kq(1-kq).

For arbitrary finite nonnegative s(N)=o(N), the tail envelope eta_m=sup_(N>=mn)s(N)/N tends to zero. Choosing k_m=ceil(4(n+1)m sqrt(eta_m)+2sqrt(m)) keeps k=o(m) and leaves enough control rarity to meet I(F)<=2^-s(N). The retained seed ratio tends to B(f)/I(f). Class inclusion gives the reverse bound. Thus every fixed-tail cutoff supremum equals K_E in extended reals. Constants require no seed ratio; the construction itself still makes sense for them. Infinite K_E is handled seed by seed. No universal finite constant follows. A099 supplies no repair to A090's theorem; A090's omitted positive pure-control correction was in its own preliminary checkpoint, already corrected before publication. A099 v2 retained that term correctly.

For the normalized derivative reference mu_i(T)=p_(T+i)/d_i and r_i(T)=p_T+p_(T+i), both laws sum to one. The joint laws P(i,T)=p_(T+i)/I and Q(i,T)=(d_i/I)r_i(T) retain their complete normalizer. Their exact identity is

    L=sum_i d_i log2(1/d_i)-I D(P||Q).

Here A096's symbol M means this downward L; it is neither A036's marginal sum nor a new entropy quantity. Tensor/parity preserves each relevant seed row divergence; parity rows have zero divergence. Activation adds k to every relevant core-row divergence. Each control row has D_c=(1-q)(k-1)+q log2(q/d), d=(1-q)^2+q^2. Therefore

    I(F)D_F=q[mI(f)D_f+k(mI(f)+ell)+kD_c],
    L(F)=qmL(f)+k[q(1-q)+q^2 log2(d/q^2)],
    L_ge2(F)=qmL(f)+kq(1-kq).

The same cutoff proof preserves the optimal downward constant. This refunds activation's own rarity while retaining the arbitrary seed divergence explicitly. The first unsupported line is, for one finite K and every Boolean seed,

    D(P_f||Q_f)>=sum_i(d_i/I)log2(1/d_i)-K.

It is exactly the existing C018 payment L<=KI, not a new universal result. Neither actual Boolean convolution nor a nonreusing terminal reserve has yet paid it.

A097's one-shared-data-bit result is assembled at author scope. Calibrate both collision atoms empty and {s} individually, keeping the normalized complement; lift through the actual signed selector kernel, preserving reference mass on zero true fibers. Posterior mismatch is exactly zero, root KL is the average calibrated child KL, and CE_root<=average calibrated complete child costs+(2+4/ln2)Inf_selector. Shared data influence is included once by averaging child influences. The complete terminal bills remain hypotheses. For unrestricted overlap, selecting an even-k bent sign and its point flip has selector influence 2^-k and entropy-drop/selector-influence >=k-log2(16/9). This refutes only an absolute selector-only extension; total core entropy and influence remain. The first unpaid continuation needs a named, nonreused terminal reserve.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

CANDIDATE_MANUSCRIPT.tex is the complete single source. STAGE4_ADDITIONS.tex records the new proof sections for review but is not an external TeX input. All prior proved reductions and four named open conjectures are preserved.

The new independently reconstructed selector application folds d to d*=min(d,1-d) by negating one child, preserving spectra and entropy drop while permuting the actual selector kernel. At the 2^t possible shared-only collision rows, conditional means in [-1,1] imply |a_T-b_T|<=2d*. Parseval gives total chosen-selector-sector collision mass delta_C<=min(d*,2^t d*^2). Binary-entropy concavity, including an added zero-minority dummy fiber for unused mass, gives B_C<=h2(delta_C). The sector need not be the smaller sector on every individual fiber; the total cap and Jensen argument remain exact.

For d*>=2^-t, the bound h2(d*)<=d*log2(e/d*) gives B_C/d*<=t+1/ln2. For rarer selectors put x=2^t d*<=1; then B_C/d*<=x log2(e 2^t/x^2)<=t+1/ln2. The derivative proving the last inequality is ln(2^t)-1-2ln x>0 because t>=2. The noncollision bill is <=2d* and JS is <=2d*/ln2. Thus Delta<=(t+2+3/ln2)d* in all parameter regimes, including d*=0 by exact equality. Calibration at every shared-only atom and the retained complement yields a single normalized complete reference with CE_root=average calibrated terminal CE+Delta and root KL=average calibrated terminal KL. Conditioning on the selector counts every shared physical influence once. The bent/point-flip lower bound, with one dummy shared bit for odd t, gives t-1-log2(16/9)<=kappa_t<=t+2+3/ln2 and hence kappa_t/t->1. This improves the previous valid exponential upper estimate; it does not refute it or give an absolute selector-only tax.

The complete conditional-chain identity is saved explicitly:

    L_i(C)=d_i[D(mu_i||r_i)-D(Cmu_i||Cr_i)]
          =d_i sum_c mu_i(c)D(mu_i(.|c)||r_i(.|c)).

A001's fixed-degree quotient keeps all residual atoms of size <=L individually and merges the rest into one tail. On actual full-support G_n=f_n^tensor(m), f_n the n-bit point defect, e=2^(1-n), m=2^floor(n/2), n>=max(6,2L+2), every influence is e, I=mn e->0, H/I<4 and true downward L/I<=2. The low-cell derivative mass is at most beta=e sum_(j<=L)binom(n-1,j), and each likelihood ratio <=1/e. The complete reference tail has mass >=me/4 from one high-degree spectator and all other spectators empty. Therefore the coarsened price/I is at least floor(n/2)-2-beta(n-1), which diverges. The exact loss is the within-tail conditional KL. This is a normalized-reference no-go even without auxiliary parity or dummy coordinates, not an FEI counterexample. Its full proof is assembled at author scope, with no claimed independent A099 acceptance based on another agent's census.

A096's opposite binary quotients are also fully assembled. Pair-parity substitution injectively relabels Fourier support. A duplicated row has complete laws r(T,0)=A_T, r(T,1)=B_T and mu(T,0)=0, mu(T,1)=B_T/d_i. Keeping only the partner gives (0,1) versus (1-d_i,d_i), preserving the entire KL. Deleting the partner loses exactly the original seed row's downward price. On an even-d bent/flip selector this is between delta(d-1) and delta d per duplicated row, while both relevant pair influences are delta and whole H/I<=3. A one-bit quotient alphabet does not bound the lost divergence. A073 independently accepts the full original scoped claim.

A073's positive-erasure gate variant F_d=b+1_(y=0)(q(u,v)-1), with q=-1 only at (1,1), has every Fourier atom positive. Retaining the partner gives complete laws (1/2,1/2) versus (1-e,e), e=delta/2. Its full KL is binary KL plus D0/2. The exact weighted quotient loss is eD0/2, with 0<D0<=log2(7/5). The integer certificate 7^128<2^63*5^128 proves each loss/e<63/256=1/4-1/256 for every even d. Both losses can be paid once by a dedicated tenth of one core influence. This fraction is reserved only for these losses. The retained row price/e nevertheless exceeds d/2+3/4, so no full row payment or globally free terminal reserve follows. A098 accepts every original clause at its explicit-family scope.

The first unsupported universal line is unchanged:

    sum_i d_i D(mu_i||r_i)
        >=sum_i d_i log2(1/d_i)-K I(f)

for one finite K and every actual Boolean seed, retaining complete normalized references. Equivalently, the all-dimensional R_ge2<=K_E I leaf still lacks any finite value of K_E. Tax-tree correction diagnostic (new scoped obstruction): set f_n=1-2*1{x=0^n} and tau=1-h2(1-1/sqrt(2)). Each coordinate has one pivotal undirected edge, so I_i=2/2^n and I=2n/2^n. In any zero-error singleton query tree the unique all-zero exceptional path must reveal all n coordinates: otherwise a completion with one unqueried bit equal to one has the opposite output. Its depth-j node has physical probability 2^-j. Those n nodes alone force expected query count >=sum_{j=0}^{n-1}2^-j=2-2^(1-n); querying in any order and stopping each constant branch attains equality. Randomized exact policies are mixtures and obey the same lower bound. Hence the unmodified additive tau bill/I is >=tau(2^n-1)/n.

The binary entropy derivative satisfies h2''(t)=-1/(ln(2)t(1-t))<=-4/ln(2). Integrate around 1/2 to obtain 1-h2(t)>=2(t-1/2)^2/ln(2). At t=1-1/sqrt(2), tau>=(3-2sqrt(2))/(2ln(2))>1/14, since sqrt(2)<10/7 and ln(2)<1. Therefore the bill/I diverges even for the best computing tree. This closes only the unmodified per-query tau payment mechanism. The stronger uniform J<=M bound has zero tax; min{tau,Delta} also has zero uniform tax. Neither is refuted, nor is the actual entropy telescope. Point defects have bounded Shannon H/I (seed formula tends to 4), so this is not a counterexample to FEI.
